## Fixed execution rules

- Execute tasks in task-ID order. Do not replan, create a sibling remediation plan, or widen issue #400 beyond the breadcrumb selector and the minimum shared dispatcher/readiness/lifecycle seams required by the seven confirmed findings, the supplied runtime refinement of the UI-thread finding, and the explicitly approved branch-scope SpamBayes correction in P8-T45 through P8-T54.
- Use only automated deterministic verification. Do not use live Outlook, a live WebView/UI, screenshots, user interaction, manual QA, manual bootstrap, wall-clock sleeps, temporary files, network access, or externally launched acceptance processes.
- Preserve all current worktree changes and evidence. Do not reset, discard, rewrite, or silently overwrite pre-existing work. New evidence receives a current UTC `yyyy-MM-ddTHH-mm` suffix and is stored only under this feature's `evidence/baseline`, `evidence/regression-testing`, or `evidence/qa-gates` directories.
- Every evidence artifact, including read-only inventories and analyses, must contain `Timestamp:`, a fully expanded exact `Command:` or exact deterministic inspection command, a numeric `EXIT_CODE:`, and an `Output Summary:`. Each baseline and final-QA command receives its own evidence artifact. Coverage artifacts must record numeric repository, changed/new-line, type, and measurable-member values where required.
- A task tagged `[expect-fail]` passes only when each named newly added regression or explicitly strengthened assertion seam is discovered and fails for the intended pre-fix assertion. A build failure, missing test, unrelated failure, crash, timeout, or zero-test run does not satisfy failure-first evidence.
- Each implementation batch is delegated to the typed C# engineer with the exact source/test scope stated in the task. Every editing batch is capped at three production C# sources and three test C# sources; the earlier four-production-source P5 handoff is historical noncompliant state that P5-T18/P5-T19 partition through read-only ownership reconciliation only. No editing handoff starts before the P5-T20 recovery and P5-T22-P5-T28 failure-first sequence; all later edits occur only in the explicitly listed bounded correction batches. Any scope expansion requires an in-place plan revision before editing the additional file.
- New focused C# files are intentional where existing production files are near the 500-line limit. Every new C# file must be at most 500 lines, reach at least 90% measurable line/member coverage, and have exactly one adjacent legacy-project `Compile` include. Every modified production/test C# file and the modified HTML resource must remain at most 500 lines. Do not add runtime behavior to generated `QuickFiler/Viewers/ItemViewer.Designer.cs`.
- Do not add packages, persisted settings, class-level coverage exclusions, test filters that omit first-party assemblies, or threshold/canonical-coverage-config changes. P8-T36 through P8-T43 may create only one output-adjacent, auto-cleaned effective coverage settings file that retains every canonical exclusion and adds exactly one `.*\.Test\.dll$` instrumentation-only `ModulePath`; every discovered test assembly must still be passed to VSTest unchanged, and `coverage.config` must remain byte-for-byte unchanged. Do not weaken repository coverage `>=80%`, new/changed measurable selector type/member coverage `>=90%`, or changed-line no-regression requirements.
- P5 may use one deterministic filtered `dotnet-coverage` plus VSTest run over the exact P5 QuickFiler test classes to establish current P5 helper/method coverage without executing not-yet-implemented P6/P7 regressions. That focused artifact is not repository-wide evidence and cannot satisfy or replace P9-T4; only the post-P6/P7 full-repository run may establish final repository coverage and no-regression compliance.
- Keep `FolderRow.Score`, `PercentageFormatter`, exact folder output paths, `GetSelectedFolder`, `SetFolderSelectedItem`, `FolderContains`, issue #398 atomic replacement, existing Left/Right behavior, popup ownership/placement/readiness, focus, themes, accessibility, and public `IItemViewer` signatures intact.
- Do not hold the router synchronization lock across `await`, provider work, UI/WebView dispatch, event publication, logging, or host callbacks. Pure/provider work may run off-thread; every production request into `CoreWebView2` and every UI-owned host callback must cross the owning UI dispatcher.
- Do not infer that an asynchronous popup operation remains UI-bound because it began on the owner thread or because `SynchronizationContext.Current` happened to be present. WebView2/WinForms creation, initialization, navigation, sizing, attachment, show, focus, close, disposal, anchor/placement callbacks, and failure rollback must explicitly re-enter the captured owning boundary after every await before touching a control.
- If any batch-local formatter changes a file or any analyzer, nullable, or focused-test command fails, correct only within that batch's approved scope and restart that batch's verification sequence from its formatter task with a fresh evidence suffix.

The P8-T20 through P8-T26 62-path formatter exclusion of `SpamBayes.Actions.cs` is historical evidence only. P8-T45 proves that commit `1cd21eb4` is in `origin/main..HEAD`; P8-T50 records the replacement branch-scope formatter ledger, which includes `UtilitiesCS/EmailIntelligence/ClassifierGroups/SpamBayes/SpamBayes.Actions.cs` and `UtilitiesCS.Test/EmailIntelligence/SpamBayesActionsRegressionTests.cs`. P9 must use that post-correction ledger, not the historical 62-path set. `coverage.config` and `.csharpierignore` remain protected policy files; the branch-scope source is no longer protected from planned correction.
- Anti-masking rule for every determinism correction from P5-T172 onward: a test that fails only under some execution composition must be made deterministic under BOTH instrumented (`dotnet-coverage`) and uninstrumented execution, and the correction must not weaken or delete any assertion, must not relax any expectation, must not add `Thread.Sleep`, `Task.Delay`, wall-clock waits, retry loops, or timing thresholds, must not add `[DoNotParallelize]`, `[Ignore]`, or a category-based skip as the fix, and must not narrow a test filter or add a coverage/test exclusion. This is required by `.claude/rules/csharp.md`, which lists "Weakening assertions or relaxing test expectations to make tests pass" and "Adding sleeps, retries, or timing hacks to mask flaky behavior" among prohibited behaviors, and by `.claude/rules/general-unit-test.md`, which requires determinism and bans `Thread.Sleep`, `Task.Delay`, and real wall-clock waits in test code. Correcting a test is permitted only when a read-only diagnosis has first proven that no production defect explains the failure; where a production defect does explain it, the production defect must be fixed and correcting only the test is a Blocking finding. The same anti-masking rule binds the P5-T185 through P5-T212 numeric branch-coverage correction without modification: coverage shortfalls are closed only by new deterministic tests that assert real behavior at the uncovered branch, and never by weakening or deleting an assertion, adding a sleep, delay, wall-clock wait, retry loop, or timing threshold, adding `[DoNotParallelize]`, `[Ignore]`, or a category-based skip, narrowing the 17-class filter, adding or widening a coverage or test exclusion, or changing any threshold or `coverage.config` value. A test whose only purpose is to execute an uncovered line without asserting the behavior at that branch is not acceptable coverage evidence. Removing genuinely-unreachable dead production code to legitimately raise coverage is expressly NOT one of these prohibited masking actions and is distinguished from them: the P5-T202 through P5-T209 batch removes the provably-unreachable inner recovery `catch` at `QuickFiler/Viewers/BreadcrumbDropDownOpenLifetime.cs` lines 153-156 — unreachable because `HandleOpenFailureAsync` routes every rollback failure to `BreadcrumbUiDispatcher.Report`, which cannot throw for a non-null exception, so the wrapped `await` can never throw — which raises `<CompleteOpenAsync>d__16` from 24/28 to 24/24 by shrinking the denominator, changes no assertion, and preserves the reachable failure and `finally` behavior; adding a coverage exclusion, documenting a 24/28 carve-out, or making `Report`/`HandleOpenFailureAsync` rethrow to force the catch reachable are all rejected in favor of removal.
- If any final-QA command changes a file or fails, invalidate the current final-pass evidence set and restart Phase 9 from P9-T1. `EXIT_CODE: SKIPPED` is never a passing result for a planned command.
- Leave a checklist item unchecked whenever required evidence is absent, incomplete, contradictory, stale for current HEAD/worktree state, or reports a nonzero exit code, except for a correctly satisfied `[expect-fail]` task.
